# Honda CRF: aftermarket parts research catalog

Research candidates gathered from public web sources (manufacturer pages, dealers, retailers, magazine tests, testers, forums). Each product keeps its source and a confidence: high = the manufacturer lists the part number and fitment; medium = a dealer or major retailer does; unverified = inferred or title-only. Nothing here is verified evidence, nothing is shown to riders yet, and no fitment, price or performance claim should be repeated without checking the source. Performance claims are the maker's or tester's, with their conditions.

Files: bars_clamps.json, bars_clamps2.json, brakes2.json, brakes_controls.json, cg-honda.json, chains_wheels.json, chassis.json, chassis2.json, cooling_valvetrain.json, drivetrain.json, engine.json, engine2.json, engine3.json, engine4.json, engine_services.json, honda-research.json, house_brands.json, house_brands2.json, ms-honda.json, p4-bearings-mounts.json, p4-gapfill.json, p5-research.json, suspension.json, suspension2.json, tuning.json, valvetrain2.json, wheels_pegs.json. Built by scripts/aftermarket/build_catalog.py.

Counts: 7013 products, 25340 part numbers, 56 rider/tester findings, 7 project-bike builds, 32 of 32 in-scope bike-years with at least one mapped product.

## Project-bike builds

### [Racer X Films: 2022 Honda CRF450R Garage Build](https://racerxonline.com/2022/09/09/racer-x-films-2022-honda-crf450r-garage-build) — Honda CRF450R 2022, Racer X 2022-09-09

- engine: XPR High-compression piston, hand-ported head, titanium valves, carbon intake runner, custom cam — Engine performance
- ecu: Vortex (via XPR) Vortex ECU with custom maps — Fueling/power delivery
- fuel: VP Racing T4 — Race fuel
- exhaust: Akrapovič Full system — 
- drivetrain: Renthal 13/49 sprockets — Gearing (stock ratio)
- controls: Renthal 827 FatBar — 
- suspension: Factory Connection Fork/shock revalve + linkage — More compliant chassis vs stock 'rigid-feeling chassis and soft suspension setting'; found: Bike 'makes you want to ride more'
- tires: Dunlop MX3S front 13.5 psi / MX33 rear 12.5 psi — 
- seat: GUTS Racing Ribbed seat — 

### [Racer X Films: 2022 Honda CRF250R Garage Build](https://racerxonline.com/2022/06/17/racer-x-films-2022-honda-crf250r-garage-build) — Honda CRF250R 2022, Racer X 2022-06-17

- chassis: Ride Engineering 23.5mm offset rubber-mounted split clamps, one-piece bar mount, steering damper bracket, CRF250R/450R pull rods, axle blocks — 'gain some torque and get a little more of a planted feel' (build goal)
- brakes: Ride Engineering 270 front rotor kit, billet caliper, braided lines — Braking
- suspension: Race Tech Fork/shock revalve, Gold Valves, springs; Showa damper revalved — 
- engine: Race Tech CNC porting, five-angle valve job, back-cut intake valves — Low-end torque
- exhaust: Akrapovič Evolution full exhaust — 
- drivetrain: Sunstar 13/49 sprockets + chain — Gearing
- tires: Bridgestone X31 front / X20 rear — 
- other: DT1 / Maxima / AntiGravity / ODI / SDG Air filter, 10W-40 oil, lithium battery, RC4 bars + lock-on grips, ribbed cover — 

### [Racer X Films: 2024 Honda CRF450RX Garage Build (trail / occasional GP)](https://racerxonline.com/2024/03/14/racer-x-films-2024-honda-crf450rx-garage-build) — Honda CRF450RX 2024, Racer X 2024-03-14

- chassis: Works Chassis Lab Engine hangers — Comfort in 'stiff-feeling Honda chassis'; found: 'improved traction and a planted feel'
- ecu: Vortex / Twisted Development Vortex ECU, TD custom map — 'massage the engine character'
- drivetrain: Pro X 14T front / 50T rear, Gold X-ring chain — 
- suspension: AHM Factory Services Off-road valving/setup — 
- exhaust: FMF RCT header + 4.1 muffler — 
- clutch: Rekluse TorqDrive plates + outer cover — 'more durability from the Honda clutch'
- cooling: ICW / Tusk Radiator braces, fan mount / universal fan — Off-road cooling
- fuel: IMS 2.2 gal tank, footpegs — Range
- protection: P3 Carbon / Works Connection Carbon skid plate; rear master/caliper guards — 
- tires: Dunlop AT82 front/rear — 

### [Swapmoto Live: 2024 Honda CRF250R Budget Build (Jay Clark)](https://swapmotolive.com/2024-honda-crf250r-budget-build/features/mod-squad) — Honda CRF250R 2024, Swapmoto Live 2024-04 (from reader comments)

- ecu: Twisted Development Custom ECU map — Minimal engine mods; found: 'runs cleaner with more power'
- suspension: AHM Valving/setup — ; found: Improved feel with hangers
- chassis: Works Chassis Lab Engine hangers — ; found: Improved feel
- clutch: Rekluse TorqDrive plates + outer cover — Durability
- exhaust: FMF Header + muffler — 
- drivetrain: Pro X Sprockets + Gold X-ring chain — 
- controls: Works Connection Elite clutch perch, Pro Launch holeshot device, rotating bar clamp, axle blocks — 
- tires: Dunlop MX34 — 

### [2026 Honda CRF250R Works Edition (factory 'build' over base model)](https://www.cyclenews.com/2026/05/article/2026-honda-crf250r-works-edition-review/) — Honda CRF250R 2026, Cycle News 2026-05-30

- exhaust: Yoshimura RS-12 titanium full system — Power; found: Sharper throttle, harder midrange, longer over-rev (combined with porting/ECU)
- suspension: Showa Kashima fork tubes, 18mm shock shaft (was 16mm), revised internals — Hold-up/consistency; found: More compliant, better deep-stroke hold-up
- engine: Honda / Twin Air Hand-ported head, ECU tune, TwinAir filter — 
- clutch: Nissin Hydraulic clutch (base bike is cable) — ; found: Light, consistent pull
- wheels/tires: DID / Dunlop LT-X rims, Geomax MX33 — ; found: +2 lb claimed

### [MXA: Five Steps to Honda CRF250 Motocross Nirvana (2018-2020 generation; tested on 2019)](https://motocrossactionmag.com/five-steps-to-honda-crf250-motocross-nirvana) — Honda CRF250R 2019, Motocross Action 2019-10-14

- exhaust: Pro Circuit T-6 dual full system — Low-end; found: 'significant gain in torque from zero to a quarter throttle'
- intake: - Remove backfire screen (use Twin Air filter + DT-1 oil) — Airflow; found: Felt through powerband
- drivetrain: SuperSprox 49T rear (stock 13/48) — Gearing; found: Less downshifting, stay in higher gear
- ecu: Twisted Development Stock ECU remap — ; found: Power spread across the curve, better mid-top
- fuel: VP T4 (97 octane) — ; found: Better throttle response

### [ADB long-term 2025 Honda CRF250R (installments 1-2)](https://adbmag.com.au/?p=51338) — Honda CRF250R 2025, Australasian Dirt Bike (ADB) 2025-04-19

- ecu: GET (Athena) GET SX1-type ECU from the 2024 CRF250R kit, remapped on the dyno, Wi-Fi map switching — Low-end torque; maps for hard-pack, mud and sand; found: 2024 map did not suit the 2025 airbox; remapped to ~14:1 AFR, no on-track changes needed
- intake:  Airbox backfire screen removed, larger hole cut in airbox cover — Airflow; found: Improved engine response
- controls: ODI Lock-on grips — Comfort (stock grips criticised)
- ecu: GET (Athena) Second injector (from GET 2024 kit) - planned — Fueling; found: Not yet reported

## Gaps and work not done

- (honda-research.json) FMF maker site (fmfracing.com) could not be fetched (permission timeout); FMF part numbers come only from dealer pages.
- (honda-research.json) No HRC Setting Tool / HRC ECU kit pages found for 2020+ models; honda.co.jp and hondaracingcorporation.com setting-tool pages only cover 2009-2017 CRF450R (PNs 38772-NX7-040, 38880-N1C-770, 38773-N1C-C00). 2021+ HRC tool status unknown.
- (honda-research.json) Rekluse maker page lists RMS-7114 'Honda CRF450R' without years; dealer sells RMS-7101102 for 2021-2026. Not reconciled.
- (honda-research.json) Hinson HC597-2101 fitment unconfirmed (2021-2023 vs 2021-2025).
- (honda-research.json) No maker-verified part numbers for Wiseco, JE, Pro-X, Hot Cams for 2021+ CRF450R or 2022+ CRF250R (search results returned pre-2021 items only).
- (honda-research.json) Nothing found for HGS, Arrow, Showa/Enzo, Race Tech spring PNs, Technical Touch, Luxon, TM Designworks (2021+ PNs), Works Connection PNs, Twisted Development flash pricing.
- (honda-research.json) 2027 CRF450R: all-new engine, frame, clutch, exhaust and 160mm rear sprocket mount - no aftermarket fitment for 2027 confirmed except Pro Circuit 250 T-6 slip-on listing '25-27' and Pro Circuit Ti-6 category '22-27' (CRF250R). 2027 CRF450RX not researched.
- (honda-research.json) 2026 CRF250R changes vs 2025 not documented beyond MXA's 'carry over'; Vital/Cycle News indicate the base 2026 CRF250R uses Pirelli Scorpion MX32 tires and a cable clutch.
- (honda-research.json) CRF250RX-specific tester feedback not found.
- (honda-research.json) Works Chassis Lab engine mount test found only for 2019 CRF450R (Swapmoto, https://www.swapmotolive.com/?p=14850) - outside scope: aluminum top hangers $174.95, titanium front mounts $119.99; front planted, smoother braking bumps.
- (honda-research.json) Dyno disagreement for 2025 CRF450R: MXA 55.34 hp vs Dirt Rider 51.1 hp (different dynos) - not normalized.
- (honda-research.json) Keefer 2024 CRF450R article lists fork spring as both 51 N/mm (table) and 5.0 (text); 2024 CRF250R shock listed as '5.0 N/mm' (likely 50 N/mm) - recorded as written.
- (p5-research.json) Target of 60-100 new products not reached: 30 new products. The shared web-search budget for this turn ran out after about 40 searches, and pages linked from fetched pages (MXA race tests 2026 CRF250/CRF450 and 2027 CRF450 tested, Keefer 2027 CRF450R/CRF250R articles, Teknik category pages, MXA tag pages) were refused for lack of fetch approval.
- (p5-research.json) No new maker-confirmed part numbers for 2025+ CRF450R from Pro Circuit (procircuit.com not reachable) or FMF (fmfracing.com not tried; FMF 2025+ numbers already in specialty/engine.json from dealers).
- (p5-research.json) Pro Circuit Ti-6 BC Pro 0312545BC: Cycle Gear's fitment block lists CRF450R/RX 2021-2026 together with dozens of other bikes (aggregated across variants), so per-model fitment remains unreliable.
- (p5-research.json) Hinson HC597-2101 fitment: TokyoMods and Firestorm AU titles say CRF450R/RX 2021-2025; Cycle Gear says 2021-2023 (existing gap in honda-research.json). Hinson's own page still not read.
- (p5-research.json) Rekluse RMS-7101102 (Core Manual TorqDrive): Ghostbikes UK lists CRF450R 2021-2024, R-S 2024, RWE 2021-2024 and RX 2021-2024 ('American models'); 2025-2026 not listed there.
- (p5-research.json) GET SX1 PRO: maker install guides confirm 2024 CRF250R (GD-MIP-0058-AA) and 2024 CRF450R (GD-MIP-0059-AA) but give no kit part numbers; ADB says no 2025 CRF250R GET kit existed in April 2025. CRF250R GET part number not found.
- (p5-research.json) No HRC Setting Tool part number for 2020+ bikes; MotoPinas mentions an 'HRC Setting tool' for the 2025 CRF250RX but hondaracingcorporation.com pages only cover 2009-2017.
- (p5-research.json) 2027 CRF450R: still no aftermarket fitment confirmed (new frame, linkage, swingarm, rear hub, airbox). Testers note the Works Edition gets Yoshimura exhaust and Hinson basket, but no part numbers.
- (p5-research.json) Teknik Motorsport items (Xtrig holeshot 70100004/70100013, Eibach 881/899/903, KYB 6001203x, Showa GPE F448HON01/F449HON01) come from shop model filters with no prices; several are shown on later-year pages than their titles state.
- (p5-research.json) Xtrig EU numbers 40101015/40101016 likely equal US 501330100501/501330101401, not confirmed.
- (p5-research.json) Not covered at all this pass: wheels (Haan, Talon, Excel/D.I.D, Warp 9), footpegs, Moto-Master/Galfer maker pages, Showa A-kit and Enzo/Race Tech Honda numbers, Ride Engineering link/clamp part numbers, P3 Carbon and TM Designworks 2025+ skid plates, Honda genuine accessories (hydraulic clutch conversion mentioned by Keefer for the CRF250RX).
- (bars_clamps.json) Renthal 932 Fatbar36 page says OE bend on Husqvarna (2016>) and GasGas (2020>) but names no models; Husqvarna/GasGas bar fitment not recorded.
- (bars_clamps.json) Renthal 2021 recommendations give bends per model without years for Fatbar36 (933/934/930/931) and Twinwall (996/997/998/999); only Fatbar 827 recorded with year 2021, low confidence.
- (bars_clamps.json) Honda CRF250RX/450RX and Kawasaki KX250X/450X not named for the 839 OE bend; Yamaha X/FX/WR and Triumph TF models not covered by any Renthal page seen.
- (bars_clamps.json) Renthal CL061 bar mount lists 'GasGas 2022' without models; 2023+ fitment for most bar mounts (other than CL063) not printed on the listing.
- (bars_clamps.json) Renthal 798 7/8" KTM SX85 and 826/822 'KTM High/Low' bends have no years; 604 renthal.com fitment text predates roster years.
- (bars_clamps.json) No 2027 models covered: open-ended '+' ranges capped at 2026.
- (bars_clamps2.json) Handlebars (ProTaper, Neken) have no per-bike or year fitment on the maker pages. ProTaper's 'Check Fit' selector needs JavaScript and could not be read, so the bar products have empty fits. Bend names such as 'Husqvarna Stock', 'Factory Suzuki/KTM Stock', 'Original YZF', 'Original RMZ' and '250/450 CRF KXF' name a model family with no years.
- (bars_clamps2.json) ProTaper VDS 022217: the title says only 'KTM 125-500/ Gas Gas/ Husqvarna' for 2023-26. Only the KTM SX models are listed in fits; KTM XC/XC-W, the GasGas MC/EX/EC and Husqvarna TC/FC/FX/TE models of 2023-26 are not itemized by the maker.
- (bars_clamps2.json) ProTaper VDS 022218: the title 'Honda CRF450R/ 04-24 CRF450R' repeats a model and may mean CRF250R 2004-24; the page does not say. VDS 022215 Yamaha shows conflicting years (21-27 heading vs 14-26 title). VDS 022216 Kawasaki covers only up to 2024 and does not name the KX250X/KX450X.
- (bars_clamps2.json) ProTaper Fuzion: the product page lists no clamp size or colours. Sport bars: no dimensions for the track-named bends. Bend chart: no pullback column on any family.
- (bars_clamps2.json) Neken triple clamps (Standard, SFS air, SFS Spring): the maker site lists fitment only up to 2019 (e.g. SFS Spring KTM SX-SXF 2013-2019 22 mm 0509060, CRF 250/450 2018/2019 0309027, YZF 250/450 2018/2019 0409043). No 2020+ roster fitment is published, so no clamp products were recorded. SFS Spring: 10 mm travel, 22 mm offset, 28.6 mm bar clamp, 7075-T6.
- (bars_clamps2.json) Neken's site appears frozen at about 2019 (its catalogue link is the 2019 Parts Europe pageflip). Current Neken 2020+ fitment probably lives in the Parts Europe catalogue, which needs JavaScript.
- (bars_clamps2.json) TAG Metals: the moto line seems to be wound down. TAG's live site (tagmtb.com) sells only MTB parts, and US retailers (BTO Sports) mark TAG moto bars and mounts 'discontinued'. Retailer listings (motostorm.it) show T1/T2/T3 bars and 'Hon/Kaw 1 1/8 100/200/300 Bar Mounts' with no part numbers or years. Nothing recorded.
- (bars_clamps2.json) Applied Racing (moto triple clamps): the Chapmoto R/S Mount set listing (SKU 08-6362SSR family, offsets 18/20/21/21.5/22/24 mm, Silver/Red/Blue/Black) shows fitment only up to 2014. No 2020+ fitment found. Nothing recorded.
- (brakes2.json) Braktec vs Brembo (GasGas 2024+): the clearest signal found is the GBrakes 280mm caliper relocation bracket (J&P), which lists every GasGas MC model only to 2023 while KTM and Husqvarna run to 2027, and the Axiom MC 450F motard kit that stops at 2023. Other listings (GBrakes GBDOS224 kit to 2025, Moto-Master 260 rotor MC 450F to 2027, Slavens Galfer pads 'all 2021-26 GasGas') ignore the change; treat any 2024+ GasGas front-brake fit as unconfirmed until the caliper is checked.
- (brakes2.json) No Braktec-specific aftermarket pad, rotor kit or bracket with a part number was found; no Braktec master cylinder or caliper is sold as an aftermarket upgrade in the results seen.
- (brakes2.json) Galfer maker tables are still unreachable: galfer.eu pages (including the 280mm kit PDF) redirect to galfer.com, and the galfer.com URL was refused by the fetch tool (not in provenance). All Galfer fitment here is dealer-sourced; no per-bike Galfer kit number for Yamaha, Kawasaki or 2023+ Honda was confirmed (KITOS3, KITOS4, KITOS10 dealer pages show no fitment).
- (brakes2.json) J&P Cycles / Cycle Gear / RevZilla (Comoto) fitment lists are truncated after a few makes and often append lines that belong to sibling listings (the same GasGas/Husqvarna block appears on the Honda, Kawasaki and Husqvarna Moto-Master kit pages). Only lines matching the listing's own make were recorded; cross-listed lines were dropped.
- (brakes2.json) Cycle Gear pages for Moto-Master rotors show no Moto-Master part numbers (retailer item numbers kept in variants).
- (brakes2.json) EBC maker part finder not reached; EBC fitment is J&P, Dennis Kirk and SportbikeTrackGear. MXS755 was dropped as a duplicate of chassis.json ch-035.
- (brakes2.json) Dennis Kirk pad entries are recorded for one bike-year (2024 KTM 250 XC) only; position (front/rear) is not stated for most numbers.
- (brakes2.json) Brembo aftermarket master cylinders/calipers, Nissin aftermarket parts, brake snakes and rotor/caliper guards: not researched this pass (search budget ran out before these queries ran).
- (brakes2.json) Moto-Master Nitro pad set SID162820 (Maciag) lists Triumph/Stark/KTM/Husqvarna/GasGas pad compatibility with no models or years; not recorded.
- (brakes2.json) Galfer GBDOS220 (J&P, 270mm non-floating + bracket, $269) is very likely the Galfer number for brakes_controls bc-001 (Slavens SKU WP17-DOS220); not added again.
- (brakes_controls.json) Galfer's own application PDF (galfer.eu -> galfer.com/moto/.../din_a-4_kits_280mm.pdf) redirected and then the fetch permission timed out, so no Galfer maker-page fitment was captured; Galfer kits are dealer-sourced.
- (brakes_controls.json) Rocky Mountain ATV/MC pages (ProTaper EVO, ODI V2 No-Waffle) returned Incapsula bot blocks.
- (brakes_controls.json) PartsEurope oversize kit pages (17040546/17040547) need JavaScript; no fitment or part numbers loaded.
- (brakes_controls.json) EBC's own site gives no part numbers on the MXS page, and its Part Finder was not checked; EBC fitment is from J&P Cycles, with lists truncated (KTM/Husqvarna TC for MXS181, Yamaha/Suzuki for MXS185).
- (brakes_controls.json) No in-scope Galfer or Braking oversize kit with a confirmed per-bike list for Yamaha or Kawasaki; Happy Trails Kawasaki kit page gave no brand, part number or fitment; Ride Engineering Yamaha 270 kit page (aomc.mx) was 'item not found'.
- (brakes_controls.json) Nissin aftermarket upgrade parts: none found in the search budget (Nissin appears only as OEM).
- (brakes_controls.json) ODI Podium grips: not found in search results; only V2 Rogue (Cycle Gear) and Emig Pro V2 (RevZilla page with no part numbers) surfaced.
- (brakes_controls.json) ProTaper EVO and Fuzion US prices not captured; Fuzion price is UK only.
- (brakes_controls.json) Ride Engineering skid plates: not found; search returned Works Connection and Acerbis instead.
- (brakes_controls.json) Cycra handguard part numbers and bike-specific mount kits not captured.
- (brakes_controls.json) Moto-Master KTM/Husqvarna/GasGas Flame kit (RevZilla) showed no part number or fitment, so it was not added.
- (brakes_controls.json) Triumph TF coverage is thin: only the Enduro Engineering TF 250-X skid plate was confirmed; no brake, lever or bar items found for Triumph.
- (chains_wheels.json) Talon: no 2022+ KTM/Husqvarna/GasGas or 2022+ Honda sprocket fitment published (TR130RL/TG328/TR113RL/TG320GL lists stop at 2021); Honda CRF250R 2020+ not listed for any Talon sprocket.
- (chains_wheels.json) Talon: Triumph TF 250/450, Husqvarna TC 150/TC 300 (2025), KTM 300 SX, KTM 450 XCF-W, Yamaha X/FX two-strokes and YZ450FX, Kawasaki KX450X have no Talon fitment found.
- (chains_wheels.json) Talon hubs: individual rear Pro-Billet/carbon hub pages for KTM (rear carbon-centred hub), Honda (TW801A/TW753A), Yamaha (TW650A/TW731A), Kawasaki (TW928A) and KTM 85 SX junior hubs/wheels are indexed by search but return 404 on talon-eng.co.uk; hub colour names are not printed on any hub page (only 'variety of colours').
- (chains_wheels.json) Talon hub kits 602KIT/616KIT/627KIT/642KIT (bearings+spacers) have no bike fitment on their pages.
- (chains_wheels.json) Regina: maker application list (Fitment-list.pdf) has only open-ended pre-2020 ranges for MX bikes (e.g. KTM SX-F 450 '14-', Yamaha WR 450 F '13-' 114 links, Honda CRF 450RX '17-'); no explicit 2020-2027 lengths, so no fits recorded.
- (chains_wheels.json) Iris: maker site lists RX and RXP motocross chains with colour only; no pitch, lengths, codes or bike applications. The German 'IRIS 520 XR G&B' (gold & black) chains seen on shops are road chains with no maker fitment page found.
- (chains_wheels.json) Talon USA site (talonengineeringusa.com) has newer per-bike sprocket/hub pages (e.g. Honda Groovelite front, KTM Radialite rear incl. Stark Varg) but is robots-blocked for fetching.

- (chassis2.json) Cannon Racecraft (revalve, lowering): no relevant search hits

- (chassis2.json) Ride Engineering link arms for 2020+ Honda/Yamaha/KTM, engine mounts: not found (only 2013-14 CRF450 linkage system, out of scope)

- (chassis2.json) Tag Metals triple clamps and holeshot device: no listings found

- (cooling_valvetrain.json) Mishimoto: the MX catalogue (mishimoto.com /fits/ and the MX/ATV hose and radiator pages) shows only dirt-bike radiators and hose kits up to about 2014 (e.g. CRF450R 2002-2008, YZ450F 2010-2013, YZ125 2005-2014). No 2020+ roster bike was found.
- (cooling_valvetrain.json) Fluidyne: fluidyne.com lists only car and race-car radiators. Fluidyne Powersports MX radiators appear only in 2009-2011 press items. No current dirt-bike line or 2020+ fitment was found.
- (cooling_valvetrain.json) CV4: no maker site was found. Dealer pages (Cycle Gear, RevZilla, JJ Motorsports) show CV4 hose kits only up to about 2016 for MX bikes (plus CRF150R) and print no colour or part-number variants. No 2020+ roster fitment, so no CV4 silicone hose colours could be recorded.
- (cooling_valvetrain.json) Psychic: appears to be an Australian brand sold by Firestorm Motorcycles. The only listings found were older radiators (e.g. WR250 2015-2018, CRF450R 2002-2008). No 2020+ roster radiator or hose kit was found.
- (cooling_valvetrain.json) Enduro Engineering: enduroeng.com lists radiator brace part numbers 11-017 to 11-8018 and guard part numbers 12-1116 to 12-9025 (12-1116BK; the 3/16 in guards are black anodised), but prints no make/model/year per part number. Dealers say 12-1116 fits '2016-2024 KTM/Husqvarna/GasGas' (Walmart title only).
- (cooling_valvetrain.json) Web Cams (webcamshafts.com): grinds are listed for KTM 450 SXF DOHC (62-011, 62-121, 62-021), KTM 250 SXF DOHC (62-082 / 62-072), CRF450R SOHC (59-912, 59-982, 59-992, 67-232) and CRF250R SOHC (57-092, 57-522, 57-312, 67-242). Spring kits VC-K41 (Kibblewhite) and VC-K52 are listed for the KTM 450 SXF SOHC. No model years are printed, so these are not recorded as fits.
- (cooling_valvetrain.json) Hot Cams / Vertex: no Hot Cams camshaft was found for any 2023+ roster four-stroke. Newest found: CRF450R 2017-2020 Stage 1/2 (dealer title only), KTM-group 250 to 2021/22, YZ450F 2018-2019. ProX YZ450F '23 exhaust valve 28.2453-1 exists, but its page and the matching intake could not be opened.
- (drivetrain.json) JT catalogue pages for Husqvarna TC 125/TC 150/TC 250/TC 300/FC 250/FC 350/FX 450 (2025) and Yamaha YZ250X (2026) show part numbers 'TBA' - no JT fitment recorded for those model-years.
- (drivetrain.json) JT catalogue has no listings found for KTM 125 SX/150 SX/etc. 2027, Husqvarna 2026-2027 MX models, GasGas EX 250 (2022-23, 2025), EX 250F/350F/450F 2025, MC 350F 2025, Yamaha YZ125/YZ250 2027 at time of check.
- (drivetrain.json) Renthal front sprocket group 492U-520 listed in Renthal application data but not found in renthal.com store.
- (drivetrain.json) Husqvarna FC 250 2024-2026: Renthal's OE-size front field shows 291--415-18P (415 pitch) while its front group is 292--520 - the front group is recorded with a note
- (drivetrain.json) KTM 85 SX 19/16 2020: Renthal chain SKU C272 (oe_chain) not found in Renthal's MX/off-road chain store listings
- (drivetrain.json) KTM 85 SX 19/16 2021: Renthal chain SKU C272 (oe_chain) not found in Renthal's MX/off-road chain store listings
- (drivetrain.json) KTM 85 SX 19/16 2022-2027: Renthal chain SKU C272 (oe_chain) not found in Renthal's MX/off-road chain store listings
- (drivetrain.json) Renthal Fit My Bike data has no records found for: KTM 125 SX 2025-2027, Husqvarna TC 125 2025-2026, Husqvarna FC 450 2027, Husqvarna FX 350 2026-2027, Husqvarna FX 450 2027, Triumph TF 250-C / TF 450-C / TF 450-X 2026, and TF 250-E / TF 450-E 2026 (Renthal lists only 2025 E models).
- (drivetrain.json) Renthal Fit My Bike data contains internal inconsistencies (e.g. CRF250R 2022-2024 rear listed 49T while JT lists 50T; KX450X 2023-2024 oe_chain C413 = R3-3 114L vs r1 C128 = 120L) - recorded as stated.
- (drivetrain.json) Supersprox application tables show no in-scope rows for KTM 350 SX-F, KTM 300 SX, KTM 125 XC, Husqvarna TC 300, GasGas MC 250/MC 250F/MC 350F and EX range, Kawasaki KX250X/KX450X, Yamaha YZ125X (Stealth) - not recorded. Triumph 'TF 250 X Enduro'/'TF 450 X Enduro' rows on RST-990 were not mapped (ambiguous vs TF 250-E / TF 450-X).
- (drivetrain.json) Supersprox 428-pitch patterns RFE-1134 and CST-277 list '125 SX 2018-2026' with no make; not recorded (modern KTM 125 SX is 520).
- (drivetrain.json) Sunstar: maker sprocket catalogue no longer online (sunstar-braking.com redirects to sunstar-mc.com, an OEM fine-blanking/stamping site with no products). Retailers checked: Rocky Mountain ATV/MC (bot wall), MotoSport (only 3 clearance variants, no fitment in page), Speed Addicts (fitment loads by script, not readable), PureMoto (opaque fitment codes). Only RevZilla closeout pages recorded; no Sunstar steel rear, front sprockets or Sunstar chains recorded.
- (drivetrain.json) Vortex: vortexracing.com returns 403; Vortex data is RevZilla-only. Vortex front sprockets and KTM/Kawasaki CAT5 aluminium pages not located.
- (drivetrain.json) Rocky Mountain ATV/MC, Cycle Gear, Partzilla and Dennis Kirk blocked automated access (Incapsula/403) - no Tusk or Primary Drive kit data.
- (drivetrain.json) RK: rkchain.com did not load; RK sprocket kits not researched.
- (drivetrain.json) Renthal chain/sprocket prices are renthal.com UK store (GBP); US pricing not captured. JT site shows no prices.
- (drivetrain.json) Supersprox prices are supersprox.com (EUR) and many variants show out of stock.
- (engine.json) Hot Cams camshafts: maker store (allballsracinggroup.com) returned in-scope cams only for CRF450R/RX 2020-2022 (single cam), YZ250F/FX 2020-2021 and KTM/Husky 250 4T 2020-2022; no Hot Cams for 2023+ KTM-group, YZ450F 2023+, KX, Triumph found. Note: hotcams.com is now an unrelated adult site - use hotcamsinc.com / allballsracinggroup.com.
- (engine.json) Vertex/Cylinder Works: maker data did not return kits for 2023+ KTM/Husqvarna/GasGas 4T or 2024+ KX450/YZ450F platforms (may exist; API query cap). Vertex 2026 news lists replica kit 24705 for Triumph TF 250-E/TF 250-X 2025-26 (not added as entry: no fit for 'TF 250-E'; TF 250-X match).
- (engine.json) Wiseco: only CRF450R/RX Racer Elite kit captured; wiseco.com blocks automated access. JE, Pro-X, CP-Carrillo: no verified in-scope fitment (JE 260955 years unknown).
- (engine.json) Web Cams, Kibblewhite: no in-scope product pages found (webcamshafts.com is legacy HTML; kibblewhite.com unreachable).
- (engine.json) Exhaust: Arrow, LeoVince, Doma, Bill's Pipes, DEP, HGS - no usable in-scope product data (HGS CRF450R 2021-2024 Ti/carbon and stainless systems exist per Firestorm AU titles, no PN/price). Akrapovič site not machine-readable; only KX450 and YZ450F systems captured.
- (engine.json) Two-stroke: Pro Circuit only YZ250 Works Champ pipe captured; FMF two-stroke pipes/silencers are comprehensive. V-Force/Moto Tassinari current in-scope kits (2020+) not verified; Boyesen Rad Valve only KTM/Husky 125 2017-2021 title.
- (engine.json) Gearing: Vortex, Sunstar, JT, Talon, Regina, Iris, RK - no in-scope data gathered (search budget exhausted). Renthal store data lists sprockets by pattern number only (no bike fitment). Chains have no bike fitment by design (520 pitch, length).
- (engine.json) Cooling: Mishimoto/Fluidyne/CV4 - no 2020+ in-scope listings found (Mishimoto dirt radiators found only for older KTM/KX). Only Boyesen SuperCooler captured.
- (engine.json) Intake: Uni, Funnelweb, Lightspeed carbon airbox - not captured. Twin Air Powerflow KTM-group 2023+ fitment is title-only.
- (engine.json) Clutch: Moose/EBC plates, KTM/Husky hydraulic upgrades - not captured. Rekluse RadiusCX for Yamaha/Honda/Kawasaki 2020+ not captured (only KTM-group and Honda TorqDrive/APEX). Hinson coverage limited to YZ450F 2023-26 kit plus title-only items.
- (engine.json) Triumph TF 250/450: only Yoshimura RS-12 (TF 450), Pro Circuit Ti-6 Pro (TF 450-C/RC) and FMF (tags) captured.
- (engine2.json) Mishimoto: only 2010-2012-era YZ450F/KX450F radiators found on mishimoto.com; no 2020+ in-scope fitment.
- (engine2.json) Fluidyne: Slavens Racing KTM page lists only up to 2016 and says 'no longer available'; no 2020+ Fluidyne radiators found.
- (engine2.json) CV4 hose kits: Cycle Gear Y-kit lists only pre-2017 KTM/Kawasaki; JJ Motorsports CV4 pages (SFSMBC47R, SFSMBC55R) did not load their fitment lists.
- (engine2.json) Arrow: no motocross (SX/MX) exhaust found, only street and CFMoto/KTM road models.
- (engine2.json) Doma: no motocross listing found.
- (engine2.json) LeoVince: X3 Evo listings found only for EXC-F enduro (out of scope); no MX fitment found.
- (engine2.json) Bill's Pipes: only KLX110-type mini-bike systems found.
- (engine2.json) DEP: only YZ250 Silver Shorty (no part number or price); KTM 2023+ DEP not found.
- (engine2.json) Pro Circuit Platinum pipes for 2023+ KTM and current YZ125/YZ250: not found (RevZilla results were older models).
- (engine2.json) Akrapovič CRF450R/RX titanium slip-on (bikebiz.com.au): page shows no part number or years; not added.
- (engine2.json) Akrapovič 450 SX-F Evolution Line (full system) and Husqvarna/GasGas OEM-catalogue Akrapovič parts: not found this pass.
- (engine2.json) Wiseco 2023+ KTM/Husqvarna/GasGas two-stroke pistons, 2024+ KX450, 2025+ YZ450F, CRF250R and YZ250F: not found.
- (engine2.json) JE Pistons: only one KTM 450 SX-F kit with no years.
- (engine2.json) Hot Cams and Web Cams for 2023+ KTM group and newer Japanese bikes: not found.
- (engine2.json) Kibblewhite: Parts Europe 'Valve Spring Kit' 09260137 had no brand or fitment; not added.

- (engine3.json) Hot Cams: still no camshaft or valve-spring kit for 2023+ KTM/Husqvarna/GasGas four-strokes, 2023+ YZ450F or 2024+ KX450. Partzilla (1051-3) and Maxiscoot (HC-3030164) pages show no fitment; the newest readable catalogue lines remain 2016-2022 (oro2u Dirt catalogue p.255).
- (engine3.json) Cylinder Works: only CW50009K03 (dealer title '2023 KTM 450 SX-F standard bore cylinder kit') found; no 2023+ 250/350, YZ450F 2023+ or KX450 2024+ kits. allballsracinggroup.com product pages for 2023+ numbers did not surface in search.
- (engine3.json) Vertex: 2023+ gasket kits and TBI piston kits were found only on Cycle Gear, which hides the Vertex part numbers; maker numbers still needed (allballsracinggroup.com).
- (engine3.json) Wiseco: no piston for the 2023+ TBI two-strokes, 2023+ 250/350 four-strokes or 2024+ KX450 was found; Wiseco 40306M09500 (450, engine2.json) remains the only 2023+ KTM-group Wiseco item. Hurreys PK1925 claims 350 XC-F to 2025 but conflicts with itself.
- (engine3.json) JE Pistons and CP-Carrillo: no 2023+ KTM 350/450, 2023+ YZ450F (beyond MX1112 in engine2) or 2024+ KX450 kits found. CP MX3033/MX3034 stop at 2023 KX450.
- (engine3.json) Athena: no 2023+ KTM-group big bore found; the Big Bore Extreme range lists KTM/Husqvarna 250 only to 2022 and GasGas MC 250F to 2023. The KX250 2023-2024 kit is already in engine.json en-251.
- (engine3.json) Kibblewhite: no maker or dealer page with 2023+ fitment; Parts Europe valve-spring-kit pages need JavaScript. Moose/Kibblewhite-style stainless valve kits (M80-80950/80960 YZ450F, M60-60850, 0926-3303) were seen with no years.
- (engine3.json) Pro-X valvetrain for 2023+ not added beyond valvetrain2.json; pro-x.com product pages were not reached this pass (search budget). Pro-X numbers here are retailer-sourced; 35.245 and 34.245 (YZ450F 2020-2022) may be truncated numbers.
- (engine3.json) Cometic: only C3806 (KX450 2021-2022 / KX450X 2021-2023) confirmed on cometic.com; C3595-EST application list looks unreliable; C3818 (85 SX) dealer specs conflict. No Cometic kit for 2023+ KTM group, 2023+ YZ450F or 2024+ KX450 found; Summit Racing's Cometic KTM page 5 lists only pre-2016 KTM numbers.
- (engine3.json) Moose Racing gasket kits: the only Moose complete kit found covers the 2017-2023 TPI generation; none for 2023+ TBI or 2023+ four-strokes.
- (engine3.json) Two-stroke top ends for 2023+ TBI engines: found Pro-X gasket kits (35.6324, 34.6324, 35.6354, 125 kit), Vertex cast pistons (250 and 300, no numbers) and Vertex 2023 gasket sets; no Wiseco, Athena, JE or Cylinder Works TBI top ends.
- (engine3.json) Search budget: the shared WebSearch limit (200 per turn) was exhausted part-way through; remaining work used only URLs already returned.
- (engine4.json) Twisted Development: no published per-stage engine build price list or dyno figures; full race engines (ClubMX/HEP spec), mini engines and 2-stroke builds are quote-only. TD sells no engine mounts or chassis parts.
- (engine4.json) Twisted Development: no Triumph TF 250/450, no YZ450F/YZ250F stock-ECU remap (TD says it cannot flash YZ or RM-Z stock ECUs), and no 2-stroke Vortex mapping. Twisted offers RM-Z only via Vortex mapping plus the RM-Z250 manual tensioner.
- (engine4.json) Twisted Development: KTM 350 Stage 1 race head has no year range on the page; we assumed the 2023-25 generation. Several TD pages have title/body year mismatches (YZ450F Vortex 2023-26 vs 2023-25; YZ450F piston '20223').
- (engine4.json) Vortex Ignitions: vortexignitions.com now redirects to TokyoMods, and TokyoMods blocks scripted access (403), so there is no official Vortex X10 model/part-number matrix for 2020-27 MX bikes. Fitment comes from tuners' pages (TD, XPR).
- (engine4.json) 2027 Honda CRF250R/CRF450R use a new harness and ECU plug, and per XPR no Vortex is available for them yet.
- (engine4.json) Jamie Ellis: his own site would not resolve. He works through Twisted Development (TD projects page), so his mapping is covered by the TD Vortex products.
- (engine4.json) WMR: Maptuner Nano is KTM-group only (Keihin 4T and TPI 2T 2016+). TBI 2-strokes need mail-in, and Honda/Yamaha/Kawasaki are listed as 'coming soon'. The per-model map list page did not render.
- (engine4.json) Hot Cams: hotcams.com now serves an unrelated site. Distributor catalog pages show no 2023+ KTM-group, 2021+ Kawasaki KX450, 2023+ YZ450F or 2021+ CRF450R cams, so Hot Cams coverage for current bikes looks thin or ended.
- (engine4.json) Kibblewhite: maker site unreachable, and dealer fitment tables are loaded by JavaScript, so no new 2020+ fitments were captured beyond what cooling_valvetrain.json already has.
- (engine4.json) Wiseco/Vertex by bike: blocked at wiseco.com and vertexpistons.com. Existing engine*.json already holds most Vertex kits. Wiseco is still missing 2024+ KX450, 2023+ KX250, RM-Z250/450, Triumph TF and 2023+ KTM 250/350.
- (engine4.json) Rekluse and Hinson: maker sites returned 403. Only dealer pages were used, so part numbers and prices are partial. Rekluse still needs model-by-model RadiusX/CX/TorqDrive kits for KX, YZ-F, RM-Z and Triumph.
- (engine4.json) GET (Athena): no new items beyond tuning.json, apart from XPR's GET-based Triumph TF450 ECU. A GET ECU/launch-control list for Honda, Kawasaki, Yamaha and Suzuki 2020-27 is still missing.
- (engine4.json) Dr. D: no ECU, mapping or engine product found. They appear to be exhaust-only, so nothing was added.
- (engine4.json) Xtrig: no engine or engine-mount products found (clamps/ROCS only, already in chassis.json).
- (engine4.json) Ride Engineering: no engine mounts or head stays exist in their sitemap. Only billet plugs, switches and a tach meter were found.
- (engine4.json) FCP Racing: the maker site (fcpracing.com) has no product JSON. Prices come from tests, and fitment for 2023+ KTM 250/350 is unconfirmed. The FCP CRF450R entry is already in chassis.json.
- (engine4.json) Works Chassis Lab: no maker-site data, and the KTM 2023+ WCL mount price is unknown. The YZ450F WCL hangers are already in chassis.json.
- (engine4.json) Luxon MX: the CRF450R, KTM-group and Suzuki engine mounts were not found (only Yamaha, Kawasaki, a discontinued CRF250R and a Ducati version are listed).
- (engine4.json) Pro Circuit: product pages carry no cam lift/duration or piston compression specs. KTM titanium valve and KTM/Yamaha clutch spring variant pages were not parsed.
- (engine4.json) Suzuki RM-Z and Triumph remain the thinnest: RM-Z has Pro Circuit parts, XPR Vortex and a TD tensioner; Triumph has the XPR GET ECU and TF250 piston, a Rekluse TF250X kit and a Ride oil cap.
- (engine_services.json) Cannon Racecraft does no engine porting or head work. Its site and 2025 application chart cover only motorcycle/ATV fork and shock springs and custom springs, so it is recorded under suspension (four 'Fork springs / shock springs' records). Its chart has no Husqvarna, GasGas or Triumph sections.
- (engine_services.json) Smart Performance: no motocross parts maker by this name was found. Searches returned only 'SMART', an Australian (Honda) rider-coaching program (fullnoise.com.au). Nothing in scope.
- (engine_services.json) JE Racing / 'Jamie Ellis': no two-stroke porting business under that name could be confirmed, so it was left out (no invented entity).
- (engine_services.json) Wilson Motorsports: no motocross two-stroke porting shop by this name was found (only Wilson Manifolds, automotive intake manifolds). Not included.
- (engine_services.json) Eric Gorr Racing: ericgorr.com now shows a parked 'domain may be for sale' page. No current maker site was found, so there are no services, years or prices to record.
- (engine_services.json) Bills Pipes: billspipes.com is now a WordPress site mixing a few legacy product titles (e.g. 'MX2 Works Pipe 02-19 YZ 250') with unrelated blog posts ('best MTB stem', 'straight pipe a truck'). It is not trustworthy as the maker's fitment source, and Bills Pipes makes exhausts (another slice), so it was not recorded. MXA tested a Bills MX2 pipe on a Husqvarna TC250 (motocrossactionmag.com/mxa-team-tested-bills-pipes-mx2-husqvarna-tc250), but year and part number were not verified.
- (engine_services.json) Boyesen Rad Valve for 2020+ KTM/Husqvarna/GasGas: not shown on their vehicle pages. Only replacement reeds PSR-042 list KTM 250 SX 17-21 / Husqvarna TC250 17-21, and the valve's PN was not found. The YZ250 'Low End Torque' Rad Valve PN is only implied as RAD34T.
- (engine_services.json) Moto Tassinari: no 2026-2027 fitment (the Parts Finder stops at 2025). The finder lookup for the 2020 KTM 150 XC-W TPI returned unfiltered results. No Honda/Kawasaki full-size two-strokes in range.
- (engine_services.json) Slavens Racing also sells a 'Slavens Mule Power Valve Tuning Kit' and Two Stroke Performance (TSP) heads, piston kits, an oversized power-valve cover and an ECU Reflash Module for '23+ KTM TBI. The TSP parts are another maker's products sold by Slavens, and the tuning kit lists no years, so they are not recorded. The '19-22 Slavens Mule carbureted 300 kit' is marked 'FULL KIT NO LONGER AVAILABLE' and is left out.
- (engine_services.json) No send-in porting service with explicit 2020-2027 bike lists was found from the named specialists. The only verified service is the Slavens Head Mod (300cc heads, 2020-2023).
- (house_brands.json) Tusk (all categories): Rocky Mountain ATV/MC is the only authorized seller and its site (www.rockymountainatvmc.com, including /docs instruction PDFs) is behind Incapsula bot protection; no authorized third-party fitment source found. eBay/Walmart listings seen were third-party resellers (e.g. Universal Powersport, MX PowerPlay) and were not used.
- (house_brands.json) Primary Drive (chain and sprocket kits, sprockets, chains): same situation as Tusk - Rocky Mountain house brand, site blocked, only reseller listings found.
- (house_brands.json) Moose Racing part numbers: Cycle Gear shows its own item/UPC numbers; Moose numbers were found only for PX1742, PX1575, H07-1909CS, MSP25023C and the handguard PNs. Cycle News article image names show 05060667/05060667B/05061204/05061204A but without fitment, so not used.
- (house_brands.json) Moose Pro Skid Plate KX450 2024: description mentions KX450X and KX450SR but fitment table lists only KX450 2024.
- (house_brands.json) Moose coverage holes on 2023+ chassis: no Pro/Pro LG plastic plate found for 2023+ KTM/Husqvarna/GasGas or 2023+ YZ450F; no shark-fin guard rows seen for 2021+ Honda, 2024+ Kawasaki, 2023+ YZ250F/YZ450F, or KTM 350/450 (list truncated).
- (house_brands.json) Moose handguard mounts (Moto Kit 0635-1738 / 53-1020 for KTM) seen on motomentum.com without year fitment - not included.
- (house_brands.json) Not searched for Moose: handlebars, triple clamps, chain guides, radiator braces, wheels/hubs, start devices (Moose catalog for these is thin and mooseracing.com was unreadable).
- (house_brands.json) Triumph TF 250/450 models: no Moose fitment seen in any page read.
